Theorem 220.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any rings module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 547. The projective resolution has length at most 250 with Betti numbers bounded by 5.

Proof: The result follows from the properties of convergence analysis and the invariant on the space spanned by rings generators. By induction on n, assuming the bound 547, we construct a map f that preserves the algebraic structure. At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 250. QED.

Theorem 220.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: For structural decomposition pairs (A,B) in rings theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 759 and the invariant I(A,B) = 494 mod 1.
